
================================================================================
FILE: .pytest_cache\README.md
================================================================================

# pytest cache directory #

This directory contains data from the pytest's cache plugin,
which provides the `--lf` and `--ff` options, as well as the `cache` fixture.

**Do not** commit this to version control.

See [the docs](https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/cache.html) for more information.


================================================================================
FILE: compose.yaml
================================================================================

services:
  # ── SearXNG (self-hosted search) ───────────────────────────────────────
  searxng:
    image: searxng/searxng:latest
    container_name: local_agent_searxng
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:8080:8080"
    volumes:
      - ./searxng:/etc/searxng:rw
    restart: unless-stopped
    security_opt:
      - no-new-privileges:true
    cap_drop:
      - ALL

  # ── Agent Sandbox (Linux workstation) ──────────────────────────────────
  sandbox:
    build:
      context: ./sandbox
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
    container_name: xalgv_sandbox
    ports:
      - "127.0.0.1:9000:9000"   # Tool server API
      - "127.0.0.1:6080:6080"   # noVNC (watch the agent live)
    volumes:
      - ./workspace:/workspace:rw
    restart: unless-stopped
    security_opt:
      - no-new-privileges:true
    cap_drop:
      - ALL
    mem_limit: 4g
    cpus: 2.0
    environment:
      - DISPLAY=:99
      - XVFB_RESOLUTION=1280x720x24


================================================================================
FILE: eval\__init__.py
================================================================================

"""Evaluation subsystem: harness, checkers, and task definitions."""


================================================================================
FILE: eval\harness.py
================================================================================

"""Evaluation harness — runs task suites and produces reports.

Each task has:
* A ``goal`` string (what the agent is asked to do).
* A list of ``checkers`` — programmatic assertions that verify the outcome.
* Optional ``setup`` and ``teardown`` shell commands.

The harness runs each task, collects results, and produces a JSON report.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import time
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass
class TaskDefinition:
    """A single evaluation task."""

    task_id: str
    goal: str
    category: str = "general"
    checkers: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    setup_commands: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    teardown_commands: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    max_steps: int = 20
    timeout_seconds: int = 300
    tags: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)


@dataclass
class TaskResult:
    """Result of running a single evaluation task."""

    task_id: str
    goal: str
    category: str
    passed: bool
    checker_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    steps_taken: int = 0
    wall_time_seconds: float = 0.0
    error: str | None = None
    agent_summary: str = ""


@dataclass
class EvalReport:
    """Summary report for an evaluation run."""

    total_tasks: int = 0
    passed: int = 0
    failed: int = 0
    errors: int = 0
    results: list[TaskResult] = field(default_factory=list)
    wall_time_seconds: float = 0.0

    @property
    def success_rate(self) -> float:
        return self.passed / self.total_tasks if self.total_tasks > 0 else 0.0

    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "total_tasks": self.total_tasks,
            "passed": self.passed,
            "failed": self.failed,
            "errors": self.errors,
            "success_rate": round(self.success_rate, 3),
            "wall_time_seconds": round(self.wall_time_seconds, 1),
            "results": [asdict(r) for r in self.results],
        }


class EvalHarness:
    """Runs evaluation tasks against the agent and collects results."""

    def __init__(self, tasks_dir: Path | str | None = None) -> None:
        self._tasks_dir = Path(tasks_dir) if tasks_dir else Path(__file__).parent / "tasks"

    def load_tasks(self, category: str | None = None) -> list[TaskDefinition]:
        """Load task definitions from JSON files in the tasks directory."""
        tasks: list[TaskDefinition] = []
        if not self._tasks_dir.exists():
            logger.warning("Tasks directory not found: %s", self._tasks_dir)
            return tasks

        for path in sorted(self._tasks_dir.glob("*.json")):
            try:
                data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
                file_tasks = data if isinstance(data, list) else data.get("tasks", [data])
                for item in file_tasks:
                    task = TaskDefinition(**{k: v for k, v in item.items() if k in TaskDefinition.__dataclass_fields__})
                    if category is None or task.category == category:
                        tasks.append(task)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning("Failed to load %s: %s", path, exc)

        return tasks

    async def run_task(self, task: TaskDefinition, agent_loop: Any) -> TaskResult:
        """Run a single task and return the result."""
        start = time.monotonic()
        try:
            run_record = await agent_loop.run(task.goal)
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start

            # Run checkers.
            checker_results = []
            all_passed = True
            for checker_def in task.checkers:
                checker_result = await self._run_checker(checker_def, agent_loop)
                checker_results.append(checker_result)
                if not checker_result.get("passed", False):
                    all_passed = False

            # If no checkers, consider it passed if the agent completed.
            if not task.checkers:
                all_passed = run_record.status.value == "completed"

            return TaskResult(
                task_id=task.task_id,
                goal=task.goal,
                category=task.category,
                passed=all_passed,
                checker_results=checker_results,
                steps_taken=len(run_record.steps),
                wall_time_seconds=elapsed,
                agent_summary=run_record.summary,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
            return TaskResult(
                task_id=task.task_id,
                goal=task.goal,
                category=task.category,
                passed=False,
                wall_time_seconds=elapsed,
                error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
            )

    async def run_suite(self, agent_loop: Any, category: str | None = None) -> EvalReport:
        """Run all tasks and produce a report."""
        tasks = self.load_tasks(category)
        report = EvalReport(total_tasks=len(tasks))
        suite_start = time.monotonic()

        for task in tasks:
            logger.info("Running task: %s — %s", task.task_id, task.goal)
            result = await self.run_task(task, agent_loop)
            report.results.append(result)
            if result.error:
                report.errors += 1
            elif result.passed:
                report.passed += 1
            else:
                report.failed += 1

        report.wall_time_seconds = time.monotonic() - suite_start
        return report

    async def _run_checker(self, checker_def: dict[str, Any], agent_loop: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Run a single checker.  Checkers are shell commands that exit 0 on pass."""
        checker_type = checker_def.get("type", "shell")
        if checker_type == "shell":
            command = checker_def.get("command", "")
            description = checker_def.get("description", command[:80])
            # Execute the check command via the sandbox.
            try:
                from local_agent.tools.base import ToolResult
                result = await agent_loop._tools.execute("shell_exec", {"command": command})
                passed = result.success and (result.exit_code == 0)
                return {"type": "shell", "description": description, "passed": passed, "output": result.output[:500]}
            except Exception as exc:
                return {"type": "shell", "description": description, "passed": False, "error": str(exc)}
        elif checker_type == "file_exists":
            path = checker_def.get("path", "")
            try:
                result = await agent_loop._tools.execute("shell_exec", {"command": f"test -f {path} && echo EXISTS"})
                return {"type": "file_exists", "path": path, "passed": result.success}
            except Exception as exc:
                return {"type": "file_exists", "path": path, "passed": False, "error": str(exc)}

        return {"type": checker_type, "passed": False, "error": "Unknown checker type."}

    def save_report(self, report: EvalReport, output_path: Path | str) -> Path:
        """Save the report as JSON."""
        path = Path(output_path)
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path.write_text(json.dumps(report.to_dict(), indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        logger.info("Report saved to %s", path)
        return path

          "path": "/workspace/backup.tar.gz"
        },
        {
          "type": "shell",
          "command": "tar -tzf /workspace/backup.tar.gz > /dev/null 2>&1",
          "description": "Archive is valid"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
}

"""XALGV — Local Autonomous Computer-Using Agent.

A local-first, sandboxed, multimodal computer-using agent with dynamic
planning, tool execution, browser automation, document intelligence,
vision, translation, and custom-model integration.
"""

__version__ = "0.1.0"


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\config.py
================================================================================

"""Centralised configuration for the local agent."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelRoleConfig:
    """Maps a logical role to a concrete Ollama model tag."""

    model: str
    context_length: int = 8192
    temperature: float = 0.6
    thinking: bool = False  # Use /think mode for this role


# Sensible defaults for an RTX 5060 Laptop (8 GB VRAM).
# Sensible defaults for an RTX 5060 Laptop (8 GB VRAM) as per project specification.
# DEFAULT_MODEL_ROLES: dict[str, ModelRoleConfig] = {
#     "planner": ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen3:8b", context_length=8192, temperature=0.6, thinking=True),
#     "executor": ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen3:8b", context_length=8192, temperature=0.3),
#     "router": ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen3:1.7b", context_length=4096, temperature=0.1),
#     "vision": ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen2.5-vl:7b", context_length=4096, temperature=0.3),
#     "embed": ModelRoleConfig(model="bge-m3", context_length=8192, temperature=0.0),
#     "translate": ModelRoleConfig(model="qwen3:8b", context_length=8192, temperature=0.3),
# }
DEFAULT_MODEL_ROLES: dict[str, ModelRoleConfig] = {
    "planner": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M",
        context_length=4096,
        temperature=0.6,
        thinking=False
    ),

    "executor": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M",
        # context_length=4096,
        # temperature=0.3
        context_length=8192,
        temperature=0.3,
        thinking=False
    ),

    "router": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="qwen3:1.7b",
        context_length=4096,
        temperature=0.1
    ),

    "vision": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="qwen2.5-vl:7b",
        context_length=4096,
        temperature=0.3
    ),

    "embed": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="bge-m3",
        context_length=8192,
        temperature=0.0
    ),

    "translate": ModelRoleConfig(
        model="qwen3:8b",
        context_length=8192,
        temperature=0.3
    ),
}

@dataclass
class AgentConfig:
    """Top-level agent configuration.  All paths default to sensible locations
    relative to the project root, and every value can be overridden through
    environment variables prefixed with ``XALGV_``."""

    # ── Ollama ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    ollama_base_url: str = "http://localhost:11434"
    vram_budget_mb: int = 8192
    model_roles: dict[str, ModelRoleConfig] = field(default_factory=lambda: dict(DEFAULT_MODEL_ROLES))

    # ── Sandbox ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    sandbox_url: str = "http://localhost:9000"
    sandbox_container: str = "xalgv_sandbox"

    # ── Budgets & limits ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    max_steps: int = 50
    max_wall_time_seconds: int = 600
    max_retries_per_step: int = 3
    max_repeated_actions: int = 3

    # ── Paths ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    project_root: Path = field(default_factory=lambda: Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)
    workspace_dir: Path = field(default=None)  # type: ignore[assignment]
    data_dir: Path = field(default=None)  # type: ignore[assignment]
    db_path: Path = field(default=None)  # type: ignore[assignment]
    trace_dir: Path = field(default=None)  # type: ignore[assignment]

    # ── Search ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    searxng_base_url: str = "http://localhost:8080"

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.workspace_dir is None:
            self.workspace_dir = self.project_root / "workspace"
        if self.data_dir is None:
            self.data_dir = self.project_root / "data"
        if self.db_path is None:
            self.db_path = self.project_root / "data" / "agent.db"
        if self.trace_dir is None:
            self.trace_dir = self.project_root / "traces"

    # ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def ensure_dirs(self) -> None:
        """Create all required directories if they do not exist."""
        self.workspace_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self.trace_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls) -> AgentConfig:
        """Build a config using ``XALGV_*`` environment variables as overrides."""
        overrides: dict[str, object] = {}
        env_map = {
            "XALGV_OLLAMA_URL": "ollama_base_url",
            "XALGV_SANDBOX_URL": "sandbox_url",
            "XALGV_VRAM_MB": ("vram_budget_mb", int),
            "XALGV_MAX_STEPS": ("max_steps", int),
            "XALGV_MAX_TIME": ("max_wall_time_seconds", int),
            "XALGV_SEARXNG_URL": "searxng_base_url",
            "XALGV_DB_PATH": ("db_path", Path),
            "XALGV_WORKSPACE": ("workspace_dir", Path),
            "XALGV_TRACE_DIR": ("trace_dir", Path),
        }
        for env_key, spec in env_map.items():
            value = os.environ.get(env_key)
            if value is None:
                continue
            if isinstance(spec, str):
                overrides[spec] = value
            else:
                attr_name, converter = spec
                overrides[attr_name] = converter(value)
        return cls(**overrides)


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\__init__.py
================================================================================

"""Agent core: control loop, planner, verifier, and state management."""


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\loop.py
================================================================================


"""Agent control loop — the core ReAct-style execution engine.

Flow:
    Goal → Plan → [Observe → Decide → Permission Check → Act → Verify] → Done?
                    ↑                                                      |
                    +──────────── Adapt / Re-plan ←────────────────────────+

Hard limits cap steps, wall time, and repeated identical actions.
Every step is persisted to SQLite and checkpointed to disk.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import uuid
from typing import Any

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig
from local_agent.core.planner import Planner
from local_agent.core.state import AgentState
from local_agent.core.verifier import Verifier
from local_agent.memory.checkpoint import Checkpoint, CheckpointStore
from local_agent.memory.store import MemoryStore, RunRecord, RunStatus, StepRecord
from local_agent.memory.working import WorkingMemory
from local_agent.models import ModelManager
from local_agent.security.budget import BudgetExhaustedError, BudgetTracker
from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionDeniedError
from local_agent.tools.base import VerificationStatus
from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are an autonomous computer-using agent operating inside a sandboxed Linux environment.
You have access to tools: {tool_names}.

## Rules
- Execute ONE tool call per turn.
- After each action, wait for the result before deciding the next step.
- If a step fails, try a different approach (up to 3 retries).
- When the goal is achieved, respond with: DONE: <summary of what was accomplished>
- When you cannot proceed, respond with: BLOCKED: <reason>
- Keep your reasoning concise.

## Current State
{state_summary}
"""


class AgentLoop:
    """The main agent execution loop.

    Usage::

        loop = AgentLoop(config, model_manager, tool_registry, memory_store)
        result = await loop.run("Create a Python hello-world project with tests")
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: AgentConfig,
        model_manager: ModelManager,
        tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
        memory_store: MemoryStore,
        checkpoint_store: CheckpointStore | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._config = config
        self._model = model_manager
        self._tools = tool_registry
        self._memory = memory_store
        self._checkpoint = checkpoint_store or CheckpointStore(config.trace_dir / "checkpoints")
        self._planner = Planner(model_manager)
        self._verifier = Verifier(model_manager)
        self._killed = False

    # ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def kill(self) -> None:
        """Signal the loop to stop after the current step."""
        self._killed = True

    async def run(self, goal: str, *, run_id: str | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        """Execute the full agent loop for *goal* and return the run record."""
        run_id = run_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self._killed = False

        # Initialise state.
        state = AgentState(run_id=run_id, goal=goal)
        self._memory.create_run(run_id, goal)

        budget = BudgetTracker(
            max_steps=self._config.max_steps,
            max_wall_time_seconds=self._config.max_wall_time_seconds,
            max_repeated_actions=self._config.max_repeated_actions,
        )
        budget.start()

        exec_cfg = self._config.model_roles.get("executor", self._config.model_roles["planner"])
        tool_defs = self._tools.get_tool_definitions()
        # Tool schemas, the system prompt and the generation reserve all share num_ctx with
        # the conversation, so only the remainder is available to working memory.
        tool_tokens = len(json.dumps(tool_defs)) // 3
        history_budget = exec_cfg.context_length - tool_tokens - 700 - 1024
        if history_budget < 1000:
            logger.warning(
                "Context is tight: num_ctx=%d, tool schemas ~%d tokens. Only ~%d tokens remain for "
                "conversation + output. Raise context_length (8192+ recommended) or expose fewer tools.",
                exec_cfg.context_length, tool_tokens, max(history_budget, 0),
            )
        working = WorkingMemory(
            max_tokens=max(history_budget, 768),
            system_prompt="",  # Will be set dynamically each turn.
        )
        max_nudges = getattr(self._config, "max_nudges", 3)
        consecutive_nudges = 0
        announce_task = True

        try:
            # ── Phase 1: Planning ────────────────────────────────────────
            state.status = RunStatus.PLANNING
            self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, RunStatus.PLANNING)

            plan, reasoning = await self._planner.create_plan(goal)
            state.plan = plan
            self._memory.update_run_plan(run_id, plan)

            logger.info("Plan for '%s': %s", goal, plan)

            # Record the planning step.
            plan_step = StepRecord(
                step_index=0,
                action_type="plan",
                decision=f"Plan: {json.dumps(plan)}",
                observation=reasoning,
                result_success=True,
            )
            state.record(plan_step)
            self._memory.record_step(run_id, plan_step)

            # ── Phase 2: Execution loop ──────────────────────────────────
            state.status = RunStatus.EXECUTING
            self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, RunStatus.EXECUTING)

            # while not state.is_terminal and state.current_step < len(state.plan):
            while not state.is_terminal:
                if self._killed:
                    state.status = RunStatus.KILLED
                    break

                # Budget check.
                action_key = f"step_{state.current_step}"
                try:
                    budget.check(action_key)
                except BudgetExhaustedError as exc:
                    logger.warning("Budget exhausted: %s", exc.reason)
                    state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                    state.last_error = exc.reason
                    break

                # Build the executor prompt.
                system = EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
                    tool_names=", ".join(self._tools.names),
                    state_summary=state.summary_for_model(),
                )
                working.system_prompt = system
                working.set_plan(state.plan)
                working.set_scratchpad(state.scratchpad)

                # Add current task instruction (not again right after a nudge).
                if announce_task:
                    current_task = state.current_plan_step or "Continue working on the goal."
                    working.add_message("user", f"Execute this step: {current_task}")
                announce_task = True

                # Get model decision.
                response = await self._model.chat(
                    "executor",
                    working.get_messages(),
                    tools=tool_defs if tool_defs else None,
                )

                step_index = len(state.history)

                # ── Handle tool calls ────────────────────────────────────
                if response.has_tool_calls:
                    consecutive_nudges = 0
                    tc = response.tool_calls[0]  # One tool per turn.
                    action_key = f"{tc.name}:{json.dumps(tc.arguments, sort_keys=True)[:100]}"

                    try:
                        budget.check(action_key)
                    except BudgetExhaustedError as exc:
                        state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                        state.last_error = exc.reason
                        break

                    logger.info("Step %d: %s(%s)", step_index, tc.name, tc.arguments)

                    try:
                        result, verification = await self._tools.execute_and_verify(
                            tc.name, tc.arguments
                        )
                    except PermissionDeniedError as exc:
                        # Tool needs human approval.
                        state.status = RunStatus.AWAITING_HUMAN
                        state.last_error = str(exc)
                        step_rec = StepRecord(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            action_type="tool_call",
                            tool_name=tc.name,
                            tool_params=tc.arguments,
                            decision=response.content,
                            error=str(exc),
                            result_success=False,
                            verification_status="blocked",
                            verification_message=str(exc),
                        )
                        state.record(step_rec)
                        self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)
                        break

                    # Record the step.
                    step_rec = StepRecord(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        action_type="tool_call",
                        tool_name=tc.name,
                        tool_params=tc.arguments,
                        decision=response.content,
                        result_output=result.truncated(),
                        result_success=result.success,
                        verification_status=verification.status.value,
                        verification_message=verification.message,
                        error=result.error,
                    )
                    state.record(step_rec)
                    self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)
                    budget.record_step(action_key)

                    # Preserve the assistant's original tool-call message.
                    working.add_message(
                        "assistant",
                        response.content,
                        thinking=response.thinking,
                        tool_calls=[{"function": {"name": tc.name, "arguments": tc.arguments}}],
                    )

                    # Add the corresponding tool result.
                    working.add_tool_result(
                        tc.name,
                        f"Success: {result.success}\nOutput: {result.truncated()}"
                        + (f"\nError: {result.error}" if result.error else "")
                        + f"\nVerification: {verification.status.value} — {verification.message}",
                    )

                    # ── Decide next action based on result ───────────────
                    if result.success and verification.status != VerificationStatus.FAILED:
                        state.advance()
                    else:
                        state.mark_retry(result.error or verification.message)
                        if state.retry_count >= self._config.max_retries_per_step:
                            logger.warning("Max retries for step %d, re-planning", state.current_step)
                            try:
                                new_plan, reason = await self._planner.replan(state)
                                state.plan = state.plan[: state.current_step] + new_plan
                                self._memory.update_run_plan(run_id, state.plan)
                                state.retry_count = 0
                            except Exception:
                                logger.exception("Re-planning failed")
                                state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                                break

                # ── Handle text-only response (DONE / BLOCKED) ───────────
                else:
                    content = response.content.strip()
                    step_rec = StepRecord(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        action_type="response",
                        decision=content,
                        result_success=True,
                    )
                    state.record(step_rec)
                    self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)

                    if content.upper().startswith("DONE:"):
                        state.status = RunStatus.COMPLETED
                        state.scratchpad = content[5:].strip()
                    elif content.upper().startswith("BLOCKED:"):
                        state.status = RunStatus.AWAITING_HUMAN
                        state.last_error = content[8:].strip()
                    else:
                        # Model responded without calling a tool: nudge it, but give up
                        # quickly if it keeps doing so instead of burning the step budget.
                        consecutive_nudges += 1
                        logger.warning(
                            "No tool call from model (%d/%d): content=%d chars, thinking=%d chars, "
                            "done_reason=%s",
                            consecutive_nudges, max_nudges, len(content),
                            len(response.thinking or ""), response.done_reason or "?",
                        )
                        if consecutive_nudges >= max_nudges:
                            state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                            state.last_error = (
                                f"Model returned {consecutive_nudges} consecutive responses without a "
                                f"tool call (last: content={len(content)} chars, "
                                f"thinking={len(response.thinking or '')} chars, "
                                f"done_reason={response.done_reason or '?'}). Likely causes: context "
                                "window too small for the tool schemas, thinking consuming the output "
                                "budget, or the model's tool-call format not being parsed."
                            )
                            break
                        working.add_message("assistant", content or "(empty response)")
                        working.add_message(
                            "user",
                            "You must call a tool to make progress.  "
                            "Use one of the available tools, or respond with DONE: or BLOCKED: if finished.",
                        )
                        announce_task = False
                        continue

                # Checkpoint after each step.
                self._save_checkpoint(state, working)

            # ── Finalise ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            # if state.status == RunStatus.EXECUTING:
            #     # Ran out of plan steps → assume completed.
            #     state.status = RunStatus.COMPLETED
            if state.status == RunStatus.EXECUTING:
                state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                state.last_error = (
                    "Execution plan exhausted without an explicit DONE response. "
                    "The goal may be incomplete. Required deliverables were not confirmed."
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Agent loop crashed")
            state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
            state.last_error = f"Loop error: {exc}"

        # Persist final status.
        summary = state.scratchpad or state.last_error or f"Run {state.status.value}"
        self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, state.status, summary=summary)
        self._checkpoint.delete(run_id)

        return self._memory.get_run(run_id)  # type: ignore[return-value]

    # ── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _save_checkpoint(self, state: AgentState, working: WorkingMemory) -> None:
        cp = Checkpoint(
            run_id=state.run_id,
            goal=state.goal,
            step_index=state.current_step,
            plan=state.plan,
            scratchpad=state.scratchpad,
            status=state.status.value,
        )
        try:
            self._checkpoint.save(cp)
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Failed to save checkpoint for run %s", state.run_id)



================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\loop_0.py
================================================================================

"""Agent control loop — the core ReAct-style execution engine.

Flow:
    Goal → Plan → [Observe → Decide → Permission Check → Act → Verify] → Done?
                    ↑                                                      |
                    +──────────── Adapt / Re-plan ←────────────────────────+

Hard limits cap steps, wall time, and repeated identical actions.
Every step is persisted to SQLite and checkpointed to disk.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import uuid
from typing import Any

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig
from local_agent.core.planner import Planner
from local_agent.core.state import AgentState
from local_agent.core.verifier import Verifier
from local_agent.memory.checkpoint import Checkpoint, CheckpointStore
from local_agent.memory.store import MemoryStore, RunRecord, RunStatus, StepRecord
from local_agent.memory.working import WorkingMemory
from XALGV.local_agent.models import ModelManager
from local_agent.security.budget import BudgetExhaustedError, BudgetTracker
from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionDeniedError
from local_agent.tools.base import VerificationStatus
from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are an autonomous computer-using agent operating inside a sandboxed Linux environment.
You have access to tools: {tool_names}.

## Rules
- Execute ONE tool call per turn.
- After each action, wait for the result before deciding the next step.
- If a step fails, try a different approach (up to 3 retries).
- When the goal is achieved, respond with: DONE: <summary of what was accomplished>
- When you cannot proceed, respond with: BLOCKED: <reason>
- Keep your reasoning concise.

## Current State
{state_summary}
"""


class AgentLoop:
    """The main agent execution loop.

    Usage::

        loop = AgentLoop(config, model_manager, tool_registry, memory_store)
        result = await loop.run("Create a Python hello-world project with tests")
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        config: AgentConfig,
        model_manager: ModelManager,
        tool_registry: ToolRegistry,
        memory_store: MemoryStore,
        checkpoint_store: CheckpointStore | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._config = config
        self._model = model_manager
        self._tools = tool_registry
        self._memory = memory_store
        self._checkpoint = checkpoint_store or CheckpointStore(config.trace_dir / "checkpoints")
        self._planner = Planner(model_manager)
        self._verifier = Verifier(model_manager)
        self._killed = False

    # ── Public API ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def kill(self) -> None:
        """Signal the loop to stop after the current step."""
        self._killed = True

    async def run(self, goal: str, *, run_id: str | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        """Execute the full agent loop for *goal* and return the run record."""
        run_id = run_id or str(uuid.uuid4())
        self._killed = False

        # Initialise state.
        state = AgentState(run_id=run_id, goal=goal)
        self._memory.create_run(run_id, goal)

        budget = BudgetTracker(
            max_steps=self._config.max_steps,
            max_wall_time_seconds=self._config.max_wall_time_seconds,
            max_repeated_actions=self._config.max_repeated_actions,
        )
        budget.start()

        working = WorkingMemory(
            max_tokens=self._config.model_roles.get("executor", self._config.model_roles["planner"]).context_length,
            system_prompt="",  # Will be set dynamically each turn.
        )

        try:
            # ── Phase 1: Planning ────────────────────────────────────────
            state.status = RunStatus.PLANNING
            self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, RunStatus.PLANNING)

            plan, reasoning = await self._planner.create_plan(goal)
            state.plan = plan
            self._memory.update_run_plan(run_id, plan)

            logger.info("Plan for '%s': %s", goal, plan)

            # Record the planning step.
            plan_step = StepRecord(
                step_index=0,
                action_type="plan",
                decision=f"Plan: {json.dumps(plan)}",
                observation=reasoning,
                result_success=True,
            )
            state.record(plan_step)
            self._memory.record_step(run_id, plan_step)

            # ── Phase 2: Execution loop ──────────────────────────────────
            state.status = RunStatus.EXECUTING
            self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, RunStatus.EXECUTING)

            while not state.is_terminal and state.current_step < len(state.plan):
                if self._killed:
                    state.status = RunStatus.KILLED
                    break

                # Budget check.
                action_key = f"step_{state.current_step}"
                try:
                    budget.check(action_key)
                except BudgetExhaustedError as exc:
                    logger.warning("Budget exhausted: %s", exc.reason)
                    state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                    state.last_error = exc.reason
                    break

                # Build the executor prompt.
                system = EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
                    tool_names=", ".join(self._tools.names),
                    state_summary=state.summary_for_model(),
                )
                working.system_prompt = system
                working.set_plan(state.plan)
                working.set_scratchpad(state.scratchpad)

                # Add current task instruction.
                current_task = state.current_plan_step or "Continue working on the goal."
                working.add_message("user", f"Execute this step: {current_task}")

                # Get model decision.
                tool_defs = self._tools.get_tool_definitions()
                response = await self._model.chat(
                    "executor",
                    working.get_messages(),
                    tools=tool_defs if tool_defs else None,
                )

                step_index = len(state.history)

                # ── Handle tool calls ────────────────────────────────────
                if response.has_tool_calls:
                    tc = response.tool_calls[0]  # One tool per turn.
                    action_key = f"{tc.name}:{json.dumps(tc.arguments, sort_keys=True)[:100]}"

                    try:
                        budget.check(action_key)
                    except BudgetExhaustedError as exc:
                        state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                        state.last_error = exc.reason
                        break

                    logger.info("Step %d: %s(%s)", step_index, tc.name, tc.arguments)

                    try:
                        result, verification = await self._tools.execute_and_verify(
                            tc.name, tc.arguments
                        )
                    except PermissionDeniedError as exc:
                        # Tool needs human approval.
                        state.status = RunStatus.AWAITING_HUMAN
                        state.last_error = str(exc)
                        step_rec = StepRecord(
                            step_index=step_index,
                            action_type="tool_call",
                            tool_name=tc.name,
                            tool_params=tc.arguments,
                            decision=response.content,
                            error=str(exc),
                            result_success=False,
                            verification_status="blocked",
                            verification_message=str(exc),
                        )
                        state.record(step_rec)
                        self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)
                        break

                    # Record the step.
                    step_rec = StepRecord(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        action_type="tool_call",
                        tool_name=tc.name,
                        tool_params=tc.arguments,
                        decision=response.content,
                        result_output=result.truncated(),
                        result_success=result.success,
                        verification_status=verification.status.value,
                        verification_message=verification.message,
                        error=result.error,
                    )
                    state.record(step_rec)
                    self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)
                    budget.record_step(action_key)

                    # Feed result back to working memory.
                    # working.add_message("assistant", response.content)
                    # working.add_tool_result(
                    #     tc.id,
                    #     f"Success: {result.success}\nOutput: {result.truncated()}"
                    #     + (f"\nError: {result.error}" if result.error else "")
                    #     + f"\nVerification: {verification.status.value} — {verification.message}",
                    # )
                    # Preserve the assistant's original tool-call message.
                    working.add_message(
                        "assistant",
                        response.content,
                        thinking=response.thinking,
                        tool_calls=[
                            {
                                "function": {
                                    "name": tc.name,
                                    "arguments": tc.arguments,
                                }
                            }
                        ],
                    )

                    # Add the corresponding tool result.
                    working.add_tool_result(
                        tc.name,
                        f"Success: {result.success}\nOutput: {result.truncated()}"
                        + (f"\nError: {result.error}" if result.error else "")
                        + f"\nVerification: {verification.status.value} — {verification.message}",
                    )

                    # ── Decide next action based on result ───────────────
                    if result.success and verification.status != VerificationStatus.FAILED:
                        state.advance()
                    else:
                        state.mark_retry(result.error or verification.message)
                        if state.retry_count >= self._config.max_retries_per_step:
                            logger.warning("Max retries for step %d, re-planning", state.current_step)
                            try:
                                new_plan, reason = await self._planner.replan(state)
                                state.plan = state.plan[: state.current_step] + new_plan
                                self._memory.update_run_plan(run_id, state.plan)
                                state.retry_count = 0
                            except Exception:
                                logger.exception("Re-planning failed")
                                state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
                                break

                # ── Handle text-only response (DONE / BLOCKED) ───────────
                else:
                    content = response.content.strip()
                    step_rec = StepRecord(
                        step_index=step_index,
                        action_type="response",
                        decision=content,
                        result_success=True,
                    )
                    state.record(step_rec)
                    self._memory.record_step(run_id, step_rec)

                    if content.upper().startswith("DONE:"):
                        state.status = RunStatus.COMPLETED
                        state.scratchpad = content[5:].strip()
                    elif content.upper().startswith("BLOCKED:"):
                        state.status = RunStatus.AWAITING_HUMAN
                        state.last_error = content[8:].strip()
                    else:
                        # Model responded without calling a tool — nudge it.
                        working.add_message("assistant", content)
                        working.add_message(
                            "user",
                            "You must call a tool to make progress.  "
                            "Use one of the available tools, or respond with DONE: or BLOCKED: if finished.",
                        )
                        budget.record_step("nudge")
                        continue

                # Checkpoint after each step.
                self._save_checkpoint(state, working)

            # ── Finalise ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
            if state.status == RunStatus.EXECUTING:
                # Ran out of plan steps → assume completed.
                state.status = RunStatus.COMPLETED

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Agent loop crashed")
            state.status = RunStatus.FAILED
            state.last_error = f"Loop error: {exc}"

        # Persist final status.
        summary = state.scratchpad or state.last_error or f"Run {state.status.value}"
        self._memory.update_run_status(run_id, state.status, summary=summary)
        self._checkpoint.delete(run_id)

        return self._memory.get_run(run_id)  # type: ignore[return-value]

    # ── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def _save_checkpoint(self, state: AgentState, working: WorkingMemory) -> None:
        cp = Checkpoint(
            run_id=state.run_id,
            goal=state.goal,
            step_index=state.current_step,
            plan=state.plan,
            scratchpad=state.scratchpad,
            status=state.status.value,
        )
        try:
            self._checkpoint.save(cp)
        except Exception:
            logger.warning("Failed to save checkpoint for run %s", state.run_id)


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\planner.py
================================================================================

"""Planner — generates and revises multi-step plans via the LLM.

The planner asks the model to decompose a goal into short, concrete
steps.  Plans are kept deliberately short (≤ 8 steps by default) to
maximise reliability on small models.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from typing import Any
import re

from local_agent.core.state import AgentState
from local_agent.models import ModelManager

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

PLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are a planning module for an autonomous computer-using agent.
You operate inside a sandboxed Linux environment with these tools:

File & Shell:
  shell_exec, read_file, write_file, list_files, python_exec

Web & Search:
  web_search — search the public web via SearXNG

Browser (Playwright inside sandbox):
  browser_navigate — load a URL
  browser_get_content — get page content (a11y tree / text / html)
  browser_click — click an element by selector or accessible name
  browser_type — type text into an input field
  browser_screenshot — screenshot the page (fallback, prefer a11y tree)

Document Intelligence (RAG):
  ingest_document — parse and embed a document (PDF, DOCX, HTML, TXT)
  query_documents — semantic search over ingested documents with citations

Design principle: "Structure first, pixels second."  Prefer the
accessibility tree (browser_get_content mode=a11y) over screenshots.
Only use browser_screenshot when the a11y tree is insufficient.

Given a goal and the current state, produce a plan of at most 16 steps.
Each step must be a single, concrete, verifiable tool action.

Respond with ONLY a JSON object:
{
  "plan": ["step 1 description", "step 2 description", ...],
  "reasoning": "Brief explanation of the approach"
}
"""

REPLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are a re-planning module.  The current plan has hit a problem.
Given the goal, what has been done so far, and the error, produce a
REVISED plan for the remaining work.  Keep it short (at most 6 new steps).

Respond with ONLY a JSON object:
{
  "plan": ["next step 1", "next step 2", ...],
  "reasoning": "Why the plan changed"
}
"""


class Planner:
    """Generates and revises plans via the model manager."""

    def __init__(self, model_manager: ModelManager, max_plan_steps: int = 16) -> None:
        self._model = model_manager
        self._max_steps = max_plan_steps

    async def create_plan(self, goal: str) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
        """Generate an initial plan for *goal*.

        Returns ``(plan_steps, reasoning)``.
        """
        messages = [
            {"role": "system", "content": PLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT},
            {"role": "user", "content": f"Goal: {goal}"},
        ]
        response = await self._model.chat("planner", messages, max_tokens=1024)
        return self._parse_plan(response.content)

    async def replan(self, state: AgentState) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
        """Revise the plan based on current state and errors.

        Returns ``(new_remaining_steps, reasoning)``.
        """
        context = state.summary_for_model()
        messages = [
            {"role": "system", "content": REPLAN_SYSTEM_PROMPT},
            {"role": "user", "content": (
                f"Goal: {state.goal}\n\n"
                f"Current state:\n{context}\n\n"
                f"Error: {state.last_error or 'Plan step failed or was unclear.'}"
            )},
        ]
        response = await self._model.chat("planner", messages, max_tokens=1024)
        return self._parse_plan(response.content)

    # def _parse_plan(self, content: str) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
        """Extract the plan list and reasoning from the model response."""
        # Try to parse as JSON first.
        try:
            # Handle markdown code fences.
            text = content.strip()
            if text.startswith("```"):
                text = text.split("\n", 1)[1] if "\n" in text else text[3:]
                if text.endswith("```"):
                    text = text[:-3]
                text = text.strip()

            data = json.loads(text)
            plan = data.get("plan", [])
            reasoning = data.get("reasoning", "")
            if isinstance(plan, list) and all(isinstance(s, str) for s in plan):
                return plan[: self._max_steps], reasoning
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
            pass

        # Fallback: treat numbered lines as plan steps.
        logger.warning("Could not parse plan as JSON; falling back to line parsing.")
        lines = content.strip().splitlines()
        plan: list[str] = []
        for line in lines:
            stripped = line.strip().lstrip("0123456789.-) ")
            if stripped and not stripped.startswith("{") and not stripped.startswith('"'):
                plan.append(stripped)
        return plan[: self._max_steps] or ["Attempt the goal directly."], ""
    def _parse_plan(self, content: str) -> tuple[list[str], str]:
        """Extract a valid, non-empty plan from the model response."""

        text = (content or "").strip()

        # Handle Markdown code fences.
        if text.startswith("```"):
            text = text.split("\n", 1)[1] if "\n" in text else text[3:]
            if text.rstrip().endswith("```"):
                text = text.rstrip()[:-3]
            text = text.strip()

        # First attempt: JSON response.
        try:
            data = json.loads(text)

            if isinstance(data, dict):
                plan = data.get("plan", [])
                reasoning = data.get("reasoning", "")

                if isinstance(plan, list):
                    valid_plan = [
                        step.strip()
                        for step in plan
                        if isinstance(step, str) and step.strip()
                    ]

                    if valid_plan:
                        return valid_plan[:self._max_steps], str(reasoning or "")

        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError, TypeError):
            pass

        # Second attempt: numbered-list response.
        logger.warning("Could not parse planner JSON; trying numbered-list parsing.")

        plan = []

        # for line in text.splitlines():
        #     stripped = line.strip().lstrip("0123456789.-) ")

        #     if stripped and not stripped.startswith(("{", '"', "}")):
        #         plan.append(stripped)
        for line in text.splitlines():
            match = re.match(
                r"^\s*(?:\d+[.)]|[-*])\s+(.+?)\s*$",
                line
            )

            if match:
                step = match.group(1).strip()
                if step:
                    plan.append(step)
        if plan:
            return plan[:self._max_steps], "Parsed from numbered-list fallback."

        # Never silently convert invalid output into a fake plan.
        raise ValueError(
            "Planner returned an empty or invalid plan. "
            "Expected a non-empty JSON plan or numbered list."
        )

================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\state.py
================================================================================

"""Agent state — the mutable state object that flows through the control loop."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any

from local_agent.memory.store import RunStatus, StepRecord


@dataclass
class AgentState:
    """Mutable state for a single agent run.

    This object is the central truth during execution.  It is updated
    after every step and used to build the checkpoint.
    """

    run_id: str
    goal: str
    status: RunStatus = RunStatus.PLANNING
    plan: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    current_step: int = 0
    history: list[StepRecord] = field(default_factory=list)
    scratchpad: str = ""
    last_error: str | None = None
    retry_count: int = 0

    # ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def advance(self) -> None:
        """Move to the next step."""
        self.current_step += 1
        self.retry_count = 0
        self.last_error = None

    def record(self, step: StepRecord) -> None:
        """Append a step to the history."""
        self.history.append(step)

    def mark_retry(self, error: str) -> None:
        self.retry_count += 1
        self.last_error = error

    @property
    def is_terminal(self) -> bool:
        return self.status in (
            RunStatus.COMPLETED,
            RunStatus.FAILED,
            RunStatus.KILLED,
        )

    @property
    def current_plan_step(self) -> str | None:
        if 0 <= self.current_step < len(self.plan):
            return self.plan[self.current_step]
        return None

    def summary_for_model(self) -> str:
        """Return a compact text summary for inclusion in context."""
        lines = [
            f"Goal: {self.goal}",
            f"Status: {self.status.value}",
            f"Step: {self.current_step + 1}/{len(self.plan) if self.plan else '?'}",
        ]
        if self.current_plan_step:
            lines.append(f"Current task: {self.current_plan_step}")
        if self.last_error:
            lines.append(f"Last error: {self.last_error}")
        if self.scratchpad:
            lines.append(f"Notes: {self.scratchpad}")
        # Include recent history (last 3 steps).
        recent = self.history[-3:] if self.history else []
        if recent:
            lines.append("Recent actions:")
            for s in recent:
                status_icon = "✓" if s.result_success else "✗" if s.result_success is False else "?"
                lines.append(f"  {status_icon} [{s.action_type}] {s.tool_name or ''}: {s.decision[:80]}")
        return "\n".join(lines)


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\core\verifier.py
================================================================================

"""Verifier — post-action verification and LLM-judge fallback.

Verification order:
1. The tool's own ``verify()`` method (programmatic — preferred).
2. An LLM judge (last resort, used only when the tool has no verifier).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from typing import Any

from local_agent.models import ModelManager
from local_agent.tools.base import VerificationResult, VerificationStatus

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

JUDGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
You are a verification judge.  Given:
- The agent's intended action
- The tool output

Decide whether the action succeeded.  Respond with ONLY a JSON object:
{
  "passed": true or false,
  "message": "Brief explanation"
}
"""


class Verifier:
    """Runs post-action verification, with an LLM judge as fallback."""

    def __init__(self, model_manager: ModelManager) -> None:
        self._model = model_manager

    async def llm_judge(
        self,
        action_description: str,
        tool_output: str,
    ) -> VerificationResult:
        """Ask the LLM to judge whether an action succeeded.

        This is the *last resort* — prefer programmatic verification.
        """
        messages = [
            {"role": "system", "content": JUDGE_SYSTEM_PROMPT},
            {"role": "user", "content": (
                f"Intended action: {action_description}\n\n"
                f"Tool output (truncated):\n{tool_output[:2000]}"
            )},
        ]
        try:
            response = await self._model.chat("executor", messages, max_tokens=256)
            return self._parse_judgement(response.content)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("LLM judge failed")
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
                message=f"LLM judge error: {exc}",
            )

    def _parse_judgement(self, content: str) -> VerificationResult:
        try:
            text = content.strip()
            if text.startswith("```"):
                text = text.split("\n", 1)[1] if "\n" in text else text[3:]
                if text.endswith("```"):
                    text = text[:-3]
                text = text.strip()
            data = json.loads(text)
            passed = bool(data.get("passed", False))
            message = str(data.get("message", ""))
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED if passed else VerificationStatus.FAILED,
                message=message,
            )
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, AttributeError):
            logger.warning("Could not parse LLM judge response: %s", content[:200])
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
                message=f"Could not parse judge response: {content[:200]}",
            )


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\main.py
================================================================================

"""XALGV — Local Autonomous Computer-Using Agent.

Main CLI entry point.  Provides commands for:
* ``run``  — Execute the agent with a goal.
* ``eval`` — Run the evaluation harness.
* ``search`` — Perform a web search (Module 1).
* ``health`` — Check connectivity to Ollama and the sandbox.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import logging
import sys

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig

logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s",
    datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
)
logger = logging.getLogger("xalgv")


# ── Run command ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def cmd_run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Run the agent with a goal."""
    from local_agent.core.loop import AgentLoop
    from local_agent.memory.store import MemoryStore
    from local_agent.models import ModelManager
    from local_agent.sandbox.client import SandboxClient
    from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionPolicy, PermissionTier
    from local_agent.tools.browser import (
        BrowserClickTool,
        BrowserGetContentTool,
        BrowserNavigateTool,
        BrowserScreenshotTool,
        BrowserTypeTool,
    )
    from local_agent.tools.file_ops import ListFilesTool, ReadFileTool, WriteFileTool
    from local_agent.tools.python_exec import PythonExecTool
    from local_agent.tools.rag import IngestDocumentTool, QueryDocumentsTool
    from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry
    from local_agent.tools.shell import ShellExecTool
    from local_agent.tools.web_search import WebSearchTool

    config = AgentConfig.from_env()
    config.ensure_dirs()

    sandbox = SandboxClient(config.sandbox_url)
    model_mgr = ModelManager(config)
    memory = MemoryStore(config.db_path)

    # Initialise RAG pipeline (optional — degrades gracefully).
    rag_pipeline = None
    try:
        from local_agent.rag import RAGPipeline, VectorStore
        rag_pipeline = RAGPipeline(
            model_manager=model_mgr,
            vector_store=VectorStore(config.data_dir / "vectordb"),
        )
    except Exception:
        logger.warning("RAG pipeline unavailable — document tools will return errors.")

    # Build tool registry.
    policy = PermissionPolicy(auto_approve_up_to=PermissionTier.NETWORK)
    registry = ToolRegistry(policy)
    registry.register_many(
        # Phase 1 — file & shell.
        ShellExecTool(sandbox),
        ReadFileTool(sandbox),
        WriteFileTool(sandbox),
        ListFilesTool(sandbox),
        PythonExecTool(sandbox),
        WebSearchTool(
            base_url=config.searxng_base_url,
            trace_dir=str(config.trace_dir),
        ),
        # Phase 2 — browser automation.
        BrowserNavigateTool(sandbox),
        BrowserGetContentTool(sandbox),
        BrowserClickTool(sandbox),
        BrowserTypeTool(sandbox),
        BrowserScreenshotTool(sandbox),
        # Phase 2 — RAG / document intelligence.
        IngestDocumentTool(rag_pipeline),
        QueryDocumentsTool(rag_pipeline),
    )

    loop = AgentLoop(config, model_mgr, registry, memory)

    try:
        result = await loop.run(args.goal)
        print(f"\n{'='*60}")
        print(f"Run ID:  {result.run_id}")
        print(f"Status:  {result.status.value}")
        print(f"Steps:   {len(result.steps)}")
        print(f"Summary: {result.summary}")
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        return 0 if result.status.value == "completed" else 1
    finally:
        await sandbox.close()
        await model_mgr.close()


# ── Eval command ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def cmd_eval(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Run the evaluation harness."""
    from eval.harness import EvalHarness
    from local_agent.core.loop import AgentLoop
    from local_agent.memory.store import MemoryStore
    from local_agent.models import ModelManager
    from local_agent.sandbox.client import SandboxClient
    from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionPolicy, PermissionTier
    from local_agent.tools.browser import (
        BrowserClickTool,
        BrowserGetContentTool,
        BrowserNavigateTool,
        BrowserScreenshotTool,
        BrowserTypeTool,
    )
    from local_agent.tools.file_ops import ListFilesTool, ReadFileTool, WriteFileTool
    from local_agent.tools.python_exec import PythonExecTool
    from local_agent.tools.rag import IngestDocumentTool, QueryDocumentsTool
    from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry
    from local_agent.tools.shell import ShellExecTool
    from local_agent.tools.web_search import WebSearchTool

    config = AgentConfig.from_env()
    config.ensure_dirs()

    sandbox = SandboxClient(config.sandbox_url)
    model_mgr = ModelManager(config)
    memory = MemoryStore(config.db_path)

    # Initialise RAG pipeline (optional — degrades gracefully).
    rag_pipeline = None
    try:
        from local_agent.rag import RAGPipeline, VectorStore
        rag_pipeline = RAGPipeline(
            model_manager=model_mgr,
            vector_store=VectorStore(config.data_dir / "vectordb"),
        )
    except Exception:
        logger.warning("RAG pipeline unavailable — document tools will return errors.")

    policy = PermissionPolicy(auto_approve_up_to=PermissionTier.NETWORK)
    registry = ToolRegistry(policy)
    registry.register_many(
        # Phase 1 — file & shell.
        ShellExecTool(sandbox),
        ReadFileTool(sandbox),
        WriteFileTool(sandbox),
        ListFilesTool(sandbox),
        PythonExecTool(sandbox),
        WebSearchTool(
            base_url=config.searxng_base_url,
            trace_dir=str(config.trace_dir),
        ),
        # Phase 2 — browser automation.
        BrowserNavigateTool(sandbox),
        BrowserGetContentTool(sandbox),
        BrowserClickTool(sandbox),
        BrowserTypeTool(sandbox),
        BrowserScreenshotTool(sandbox),
        # Phase 2 — RAG / document intelligence.
        IngestDocumentTool(rag_pipeline),
        QueryDocumentsTool(rag_pipeline),
    )

    agent_loop = AgentLoop(config, model_mgr, registry, memory)
    harness = EvalHarness()

    try:
        report = await harness.run_suite(agent_loop, category=args.category)
        output = config.trace_dir / "eval_report.json"
        harness.save_report(report, output)
        print(f"\n{'='*60}")
        print(f"Evaluation Report")
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        print(f"Total:   {report.total_tasks}")
        print(f"Passed:  {report.passed}")
        print(f"Failed:  {report.failed}")
        print(f"Errors:  {report.errors}")
        print(f"Rate:    {report.success_rate:.1%}")
        print(f"Time:    {report.wall_time_seconds:.1f}s")
        print(f"Report:  {output}")
        print(f"{'='*60}")
        return 0
    finally:
        await sandbox.close()
        await model_mgr.close()


# ── Search command (legacy Module 1) ────────────────────────────────────

def cmd_search(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Run a web search via SearXNG."""
    from local_agent.web_search import (
        SearchMode,
        SearchPolicyError,
        SearchRequest,
        SearchUnavailableError,
        SearxNGClient,
        TraceStore,
        WebSearchService,
    )

    service = WebSearchService(SearxNGClient(), TraceStore())
    try:
        response = service.execute(
            SearchRequest(query=args.query, max_results=args.max_results, task_id=args.task_id),
            SearchMode(args.mode),
            args.approved,
        )
    except (SearchUnavailableError, SearchPolicyError) as exc:
        print(f"Error: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print(json.dumps({
        "request_id": response.request_id,
        "query": response.query,
        "results": [r.__dict__ for r in response.results],
    }, indent=2))
    return 0


# ── Health command ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

async def cmd_health(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    """Check connectivity to Ollama and the sandbox."""
    from local_agent.models import ModelManager
    from local_agent.sandbox.client import SandboxClient

    config = AgentConfig.from_env()
    model_mgr = ModelManager(config)
    sandbox = SandboxClient(config.sandbox_url)

    ollama_ok = await model_mgr.health_check()
    sandbox_ok = await sandbox.health_check()

    print(f"Ollama  ({config.ollama_base_url}): {'✓ OK' if ollama_ok else '✗ UNREACHABLE'}")
    print(f"Sandbox ({config.sandbox_url}):     {'✓ OK' if sandbox_ok else '✗ UNREACHABLE'}")

    await model_mgr.close()
    await sandbox.close()
    return 0 if (ollama_ok and sandbox_ok) else 1


# ── CLI entry point ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def cli_main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="xalgv",
        description="XALGV — Local Autonomous Computer-Using Agent",
    )
    parser.add_argument("-v", "--verbose", action="store_true", help="Enable debug logging.")
    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)

    # run
    run_parser = subparsers.add_parser("run", help="Execute the agent with a goal.")
    run_parser.add_argument("goal", help="The goal for the agent to accomplish.")

    # eval
    eval_parser = subparsers.add_parser("eval", help="Run the evaluation harness.")
    eval_parser.add_argument("--category", help="Only run tasks of this category.")

    # search
    search_parser = subparsers.add_parser("search", help="Run a web search via SearXNG.")
    search_parser.add_argument("query", help="Search query.")
    search_parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["off", "ask", "on"], default="off")
    search_parser.add_argument("--approved", action="store_true")
    search_parser.add_argument("--max-results", type=int, default=5)
    search_parser.add_argument("--task-id", default="cli")

    # health
    subparsers.add_parser("health", help="Check Ollama and sandbox connectivity.")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.verbose:
        logging.getLogger().setLevel(logging.DEBUG)

    if args.command == "run":
        return asyncio.run(cmd_run(args))
    elif args.command == "eval":
        return asyncio.run(cmd_eval(args))
    elif args.command == "search":
        return cmd_search(args)
    elif args.command == "health":
        return asyncio.run(cmd_health(args))
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(cli_main())
