"""File operation tools — read, write, list, and delete files in the sandbox."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult, VerificationStatus


class ReadFileTool(Tool):
    """Read the contents of a file in the sandbox workspace."""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self._sandbox = sandbox_client

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "read_file"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return "Read the contents of a file.  Returns the text content or an error if the file does not exist."

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.READ_ONLY

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "path": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Absolute or workspace-relative path to read.",
                },
                "max_lines": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Maximum number of lines to return (default: 200).",
                    "default": 200,
                },
            },
            "required": ["path"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        if self._sandbox is None:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox not configured.")
        return await self._sandbox.read_file(params["path"], max_lines=params.get("max_lines", 200))

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.success:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="File read successfully.",
                evidence={"length": len(result.output)},
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"File read failed: {result.error}",
        )


class WriteFileTool(Tool):
    """Write or overwrite a file in the sandbox workspace."""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self._sandbox = sandbox_client

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "write_file"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Write content to a file.  Creates parent directories if needed.  Overwrites existing files "
            "unless append=true.  For large files, write the first part, then add the rest in several "
            "calls with append=true."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.WORKSPACE_WRITE

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "path": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Absolute or workspace-relative path to write.",
                },
                "content": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The text content to write.",
                },
                "append": {
                    "type": "boolean",
                    "description": "Append to the file instead of overwriting (default: false).",
                    "default": False,
                },
            },
            "required": ["path", "content"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        if self._sandbox is None:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox not configured.")
        return await self._sandbox.write_file(
            params["path"], params["content"], append=bool(params.get("append", False))
        )

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        # Verify by reading back and checking the file exists.
        if not result.success:
            return VerificationResult(status=VerificationStatus.FAILED, message=f"Write failed: {result.error}")
        if self._sandbox is None:
            return VerificationResult(status=VerificationStatus.SKIPPED, message="Cannot verify without sandbox.")
        read_result = await self._sandbox.read_file(params["path"], max_lines=1)
        if read_result.success:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="File exists after write.",
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message="File not found after write.",
        )


class ListFilesTool(Tool):
    """List files and directories in a path inside the sandbox."""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self._sandbox = sandbox_client

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "list_files"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.READ_ONLY

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "path": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Directory path to list (default: /workspace).",
                    "default": "/workspace",
                },
            },
            "required": [],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        if self._sandbox is None:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox not configured.")
        return await self._sandbox.list_files(params.get("path", "/workspace"))

================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\tools\python_exec.py

"""Python execution tool — runs Python code inside the sandbox."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult, VerificationStatus


class PythonExecTool(Tool):
    """Execute a Python script inside the sandbox and return the output."""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self._sandbox = sandbox_client

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "python_exec"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Execute Python code in the sandbox.  The code is written to a "
            "temporary file and run with ``python3``.  Use for data processing, "
            "calculations, file manipulation, and testing."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.WORKSPACE_WRITE

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "code": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The Python source code to execute.",
                },
                "timeout": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Timeout in seconds (default: 30).",
                    "default": 30,
                    "minimum": 1,
                    "maximum": 120,
                },
            },
            "required": ["code"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        code = params["code"]
        timeout = params.get("timeout", 30)

        if self._sandbox is None:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error="Sandbox not configured.")

        # Write code to a temp file and execute it.
        write_result = await self._sandbox.write_file(
            "/tmp/_agent_script.py", code
        )
        if not write_result.success:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"Failed to write script: {write_result.error}")

        return await self._sandbox.execute_shell(
            command="python3 /tmp/_agent_script.py",
            working_dir="/workspace",
            timeout=timeout,
        )

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.exit_code == 0:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="Python script exited successfully.",
                evidence={"exit_code": 0},
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"Python script failed with exit code {result.exit_code}.",
            evidence={"exit_code": result.exit_code, "error": result.error},
        )


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\tools\rag.py
================================================================================

"""RAG tools — document ingestion and query as proper agent tools.

Wraps the RAG pipeline (:mod:`local_agent.rag`) into the standard tool
interface so the agent loop can ingest and query documents just like
any other tool, with permission gating and verification.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult, VerificationStatus


class IngestDocumentTool(Tool):
    """Ingest a document into the RAG vector store for later querying.

    Parses the document (PDF, Office, HTML, text), chunks it, embeds it
    via bge-m3, and stores the vectors for semantic retrieval.
    """

    def __init__(self, rag_pipeline: Any = None) -> None:
        self._rag = rag_pipeline

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "ingest_document"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Ingest a document file (PDF, DOCX, HTML, TXT, etc.) into the "
            "knowledge base for later querying.  The document is parsed, "
            "chunked, and embedded.  Returns the number of chunks created."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.READ_ONLY

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "path": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Absolute path to the document file to ingest.",
                },
            },
            "required": ["path"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        if self._rag is None:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error="RAG pipeline is not configured.",
            )

        path = params.get("path", "")
        try:
            chunk_count = await self._rag.ingest(path)
            return ToolResult(
                success=True,
                output=f"Successfully ingested '{path}': {chunk_count} chunks created.",
                metadata={"chunk_count": chunk_count, "path": path},
            )
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error=f"Document not found: {path}",
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error=f"Ingestion failed: {exc}",
            )

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.success and result.metadata.get("chunk_count", 0) > 0:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message=f"Document ingested: {result.metadata['chunk_count']} chunks.",
                evidence=result.metadata,
            )
        if result.success:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="Document ingested but produced no chunks.",
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"Ingestion failed: {result.error}",
        )


class QueryDocumentsTool(Tool):
    """Query the RAG knowledge base to find information from ingested documents.

    Returns the most relevant text chunks along with source references
    (file name and page number when available).
    """

    def __init__(self, rag_pipeline: Any = None) -> None:
        self._rag = rag_pipeline

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "query_documents"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Search ingested documents for information relevant to a question.  "
            "Returns the top matching text chunks with source citations "
            "(file name and page numbers).  Use this after ingesting documents."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.READ_ONLY

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "question": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The question to search for in the document knowledge base.",
                },
                "top_k": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Number of results to return (default: 5).",
                    "default": 5,
                    "minimum": 1,
                    "maximum": 20,
                },
            },
            "required": ["question"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        if self._rag is None:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error="RAG pipeline is not configured.",
            )

        question = params.get("question", "")
        top_k = params.get("top_k", 5)

        try:
            response = await self._rag.query(question, top_k=top_k)

            if not response.hits:
                return ToolResult(
                    success=True,
                    output="No relevant documents found for the query.",
                    metadata={"hit_count": 0, "question": question},
                )

            # Format results with source citations.
            lines: list[str] = []
            for i, hit in enumerate(response.hits, 1):
                score_pct = f"{hit.score:.1%}" if hit.score <= 1.0 else f"{hit.score:.2f}"
                lines.append(
                    f"--- Result {i} [{hit.source_reference}] "
                    f"(relevance: {score_pct}) ---\n"
                    f"{hit.chunk.text}"
                )

            output = "\n\n".join(lines)
            return ToolResult(
                success=True,
                output=output,
                metadata={
                    "hit_count": len(response.hits),
                    "question": question,
                    "sources": [h.source_reference for h in response.hits],
                },
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error=f"Query failed: {exc}",
            )

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.success and result.metadata.get("hit_count", 0) > 0:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message=f"Query returned {result.metadata['hit_count']} results.",
                evidence={
                    "hit_count": result.metadata["hit_count"],
                    "sources": result.metadata.get("sources", []),
                },
            )
        if result.success:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="Query completed but found no matching documents.",
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"Query failed: {result.error}",
        )



================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\tools\registry.py
================================================================================

"""Tool registry — central catalogue of available tools.

The registry:
* Holds all registered :class:`Tool` instances.
* Filters tools by permission tier so the model only sees what it may use.
* Serialises tool definitions into the OpenAI-compatible format that Ollama expects.
* Dispatches execution requests and runs post-action verification.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionDeniedError, PermissionPolicy, PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ToolRegistry:
    """Stores and dispatches agent tools, respecting permission tiers."""

    def __init__(self, policy: PermissionPolicy | None = None) -> None:
        self._tools: dict[str, Tool] = {}
        self._policy = policy or PermissionPolicy()

    # ── Registration ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def register(self, tool: Tool) -> None:
        """Register a tool.  Raises if a tool with the same name exists."""
        if tool.name in self._tools:
            raise ValueError(f"Tool '{tool.name}' is already registered.")
        self._tools[tool.name] = tool
        logger.debug("Registered tool: %s (tier=%s)", tool.name, tool.permission_tier.name)

    def register_many(self, *tools: Tool) -> None:
        for tool in tools:
            self.register(tool)

    # ── Lookup ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    def get(self, name: str) -> Tool | None:
        return self._tools.get(name)

    def list_tools(self, *, max_tier: PermissionTier | None = None) -> list[Tool]:
        """Return tools up to *max_tier* (inclusive).  None → all tools."""
        if max_tier is None:
            return list(self._tools.values())
        return [t for t in self._tools.values() if t.permission_tier <= max_tier]

    def get_tool_definitions(self, *, max_tier: PermissionTier | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Return OpenAI-style tool definitions for tools the agent may see."""
        return [t.to_openai_tool() for t in self.list_tools(max_tier=max_tier)]

    @property
    def names(self) -> list[str]:
        return list(self._tools)

    # ── Execution ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    async def execute(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: dict[str, Any],
        *,
        human_approved: bool = False,
    ) -> ToolResult:
        """Execute a tool by name, checking permissions first."""
        tool = self._tools.get(name)
        if tool is None:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"Unknown tool: {name}")

        # Permission gate.
        if not self._policy.is_allowed(tool.permission_tier, human_approved):
            raise PermissionDeniedError(name, tool.permission_tier)

        try:
            result = await tool.execute(params)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.exception("Tool %s raised an exception", name)
            result = ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")

        return result

    async def execute_and_verify(
        self,
        name: str,
        params: dict[str, Any],
        *,
        human_approved: bool = False,
    ) -> tuple[ToolResult, VerificationResult]:
        """Execute a tool and run its verifier."""
        result = await self.execute(name, params, human_approved=human_approved)
        tool = self._tools.get(name)
        if tool is None:
            from local_agent.tools.base import VerificationStatus
            verification = VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.SKIPPED,
                message="Tool not found; skipping verification.",
            )
        else:
            try:
                verification = await tool.verify(params, result)
            except Exception as exc:
                from local_agent.tools.base import VerificationStatus
                logger.exception("Verifier for %s raised an exception", name)
                verification = VerificationResult(
                    status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
                    message=f"Verifier error: {exc}",
                )

        return result, verification


================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\tools\shell.py
================================================================================

"""Shell execution tool — runs commands inside the sandbox."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult, VerificationStatus


class ShellExecTool(Tool):
    """Execute a shell command inside the sandbox and return stdout/stderr."""

    def __init__(self, sandbox_client: Any = None) -> None:
        self._sandbox = sandbox_client

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "shell_exec"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Execute a shell command in the sandbox Linux environment. "
            "Returns stdout, stderr, and exit code. Use for running programs, "
            "installing packages, git operations, and system inspection."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.WORKSPACE_WRITE

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "command": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The shell command to execute.",
                },
                "working_dir": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Working directory (default: /workspace).",
                    "default": "/workspace",
                },
                "timeout": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Timeout in seconds (default: 30).",
                    "default": 30,
                    "minimum": 1,
                    "maximum": 300,
                },
            },
            "required": ["command"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        command = params["command"]
        working_dir = params.get("working_dir", "/workspace")
        timeout = params.get("timeout", 30)

        if self._sandbox is None:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error="Sandbox client not configured.  Cannot execute shell commands.",
            )

        return await self._sandbox.execute_shell(
            command=command,
            working_dir=working_dir,
            timeout=timeout,
        )

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.exit_code == 0:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message=f"Command exited with code 0.",
                evidence={"exit_code": result.exit_code},
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"Command exited with code {result.exit_code}.",
            evidence={"exit_code": result.exit_code, "error": result.error},
        )

FILE: local_agent\tools\web_search.py

"""Web search tool — wraps the SearXNG search service as a proper agent tool."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionTier
from local_agent.tools.base import Tool, ToolResult, VerificationResult, VerificationStatus
from local_agent.web_search import (
    SearchMode,
    SearchPolicyError,
    SearchRequest,
    SearchUnavailableError,
    SearxNGClient,
    TraceStore,
    WebSearchService,
)


class WebSearchTool(Tool):
    """Search the public web via SearXNG and return cited result metadata.

    This tool wraps the existing :class:`WebSearchService` into the standard
    tool interface so the agent loop can call it like any other tool, with
    permission gating and verification.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = "http://localhost:8080",
        trace_dir: str = "traces",
        *,
        search_mode: SearchMode = SearchMode.ON,
    ) -> None:
        try:
            client = SearxNGClient(base_url)
        except SearchPolicyError:
            # If we can't validate the endpoint at init time (e.g. host not
            # running yet), create with defaults and let execute-time fail.
            client = None  # type: ignore[assignment]
        self._client = client
        self._trace = TraceStore(trace_dir)
        self._service = WebSearchService(client, self._trace) if client else None
        self._mode = search_mode

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        return "web_search"

    @property
    def description(self) -> str:
        return (
            "Search the public web using a local SearXNG instance. "
            "Returns titles, URLs, and snippets for the top results. "
            "Cannot log in, download files, or submit forms."
        )

    @property
    def permission_tier(self) -> PermissionTier:
        return PermissionTier.NETWORK

    @property
    def parameters_schema(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "query": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The search query string.",
                    "maxLength": 400,
                },
                "max_results": {
                    "type": "integer",
                    "description": "Maximum number of results to return (default: 5).",
                    "default": 5,
                    "minimum": 1,
                    "maximum": 10,
                },
            },
            "required": ["query"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }

    async def execute(self, params: dict[str, Any]) -> ToolResult:
        query = params.get("query", "")
        max_results = params.get("max_results", 5)

        if self._service is None:
            return ToolResult(
                success=False,
                output="",
                error="SearXNG search service is not available.  "
                      "Ensure SearXNG is running at the configured URL.",
            )

        try:
            request = SearchRequest(
                query=query,
                max_results=max_results,
                task_id="agent",
            )
            response = self._service.execute(request, self._mode, approved_for_task=True)

            # Format results for the agent.
            lines: list[str] = []
            for r in response.results:
                lines.append(
                    f"[{r.source_id}] {r.title}\n"
                    f"  URL: {r.url}\n"
                    f"  Snippet: {r.snippet}"
                )

            output = "\n\n".join(lines) if lines else "No results found."
            return ToolResult(
                success=True,
                output=output,
                metadata={
                    "request_id": response.request_id,
                    "result_count": len(response.results),
                },
            )

        except SearchUnavailableError as exc:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"Search disabled: {exc}")
        except SearchPolicyError as exc:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"Search policy error: {exc}")
        except Exception as exc:
            return ToolResult(success=False, output="", error=f"Search error: {exc}")

    async def verify(self, params: dict[str, Any], result: ToolResult) -> VerificationResult:
        if result.success and result.metadata.get("result_count", 0) > 0:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message=f"Search returned {result.metadata['result_count']} results.",
                evidence={"result_count": result.metadata["result_count"]},
            )
        if result.success:
            return VerificationResult(
                status=VerificationStatus.PASSED,
                message="Search completed but returned no results.",
            )
        return VerificationResult(
            status=VerificationStatus.FAILED,
            message=f"Search failed: {result.error}",
        )



================================================================================
FILE: local_agent\web_search.py
================================================================================

"""Bounded and auditable SearXNG search integration for Module 1."""

from __future__ import annotations

import ipaddress
import json
import os
import socket
import uuid
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable
from urllib.error import HTTPError, URLError
from urllib.parse import urlencode, urlparse
from urllib.request import HTTPRedirectHandler, Request, build_opener


class SearchMode(str, Enum):
    OFF = "off"
    ASK = "ask"
    ON = "on"


class SearchUnavailableError(PermissionError):
    """Raised when search has not been enabled by the user."""


class SearchPolicyError(ValueError):
    """Raised when input or a configured endpoint violates policy."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchPolicy:
    max_query_length: int = 400
    max_results: int = 10
    timeout_seconds: float = 12.0
    allowed_endpoint_hosts: tuple[str, ...] = ("localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchRequest:
    query: str
    max_results: int = 5
    allowed_domains: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    task_id: str = "default"

    def validate(self, policy: SearchPolicy) -> None:
        if not self.query or not self.query.strip():
            raise SearchPolicyError("Search query must not be empty.")
        if len(self.query) > policy.max_query_length:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"Search query exceeds {policy.max_query_length} characters.")
        if not 1 <= self.max_results <= policy.max_results:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"max_results must be between 1 and {policy.max_results}.")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResult:
    source_id: str
    title: str
    url: str
    snippet: str
    provider_rank: int
    retrieved_at: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SearchResponse:
    request_id: str
    query: str
    results: tuple[SearchResult, ...]
    retrieved_at: str


def utc_now() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")


def user_has_enabled_search(mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> bool:
    if mode is SearchMode.ON:
        return True
    if mode is SearchMode.ASK:
        return approved_for_task
    return False


def web_search_tool_definition(mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return no tool at all when the user has not granted network search."""
    if not user_has_enabled_search(mode, approved_for_task):
        return []
    return [{
        "type": "function",
        "function": {
            "name": "web_search",
            "description": "Search public web sources and return cited result metadata. It cannot log in, download, upload, or submit forms.",
            "parameters": {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {"type": "string", "maxLength": 400},
                    "max_results": {"type": "integer", "minimum": 1, "maximum": 10},
                },
                "required": ["query"],
                "additionalProperties": False,
            },
        },
    }]


class NoRedirect(HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):  # type: ignore[override]
        return None


class SearxNGClient:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str | None = None, policy: SearchPolicy | None = None):
        self.base_url = base_url or os.environ.get("SEARXNG_BASE_URL", "http://localhost:8080")
        self.policy = policy or SearchPolicy()
        self._validate_endpoint()

    def _validate_endpoint(self) -> None:
        parsed = urlparse(self.base_url)
        if parsed.scheme != "http" or not parsed.hostname:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint must be a local http URL.")
        host = parsed.hostname.lower()
        if host not in self.policy.allowed_endpoint_hosts:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint host is not approved.")
        try:
            addresses = {info[4][0] for info in socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)}
        except socket.gaierror as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"Cannot resolve SearXNG endpoint: {host}") from exc
        if not all(ipaddress.ip_address(address).is_loopback for address in addresses):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint must resolve to a loopback address.")

    def search(self, request: SearchRequest) -> SearchResponse:
        request.validate(self.policy)
        params = {"q": request.query.strip(), "format": "json", "safesearch": "1"}
        if request.allowed_domains:
            params["domains"] = ",".join(request.allowed_domains)
        url = f"{self.base_url.rstrip('/')}/search?{urlencode(params)}"
        payload = self._fetch_json(url)
        retrieved_at = utc_now()
        raw_results = payload.get("results", [])
        if not isinstance(raw_results, list):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG returned an invalid results payload.")
        results: list[SearchResult] = []
        for rank, item in enumerate(raw_results[: request.max_results], start=1):
            if not isinstance(item, dict):
                continue
            result_url = str(item.get("url", "")).strip()
            if not result_url.startswith(("https://", "http://")):
                continue
            results.append(SearchResult(
                source_id=f"src_{rank:03d}",
                title=str(item.get("title", "Untitled source")).strip(),
                url=result_url,
                snippet=str(item.get("content", "")).strip(),
                provider_rank=rank,
                retrieved_at=retrieved_at,
            ))
        return SearchResponse(str(uuid.uuid4()), request.query.strip(), tuple(results), retrieved_at)

    def _fetch_json(self, url: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        req = Request(url, headers={"Accept": "application/json", "User-Agent": "LocalAgentWebSearch/0.1"})
        opener = build_opener(NoRedirect())
        try:
            with opener.open(req, timeout=self.policy.timeout_seconds) as response:
                if response.status != 200:
                    raise SearchPolicyError(f"SearXNG returned HTTP {response.status}.")
                raw = response.read(2_000_000)
        except HTTPError as exc:
            if 300 <= exc.code < 400:
                raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG endpoint redirected; redirects are not allowed.") from exc
            raise SearchPolicyError(f"SearXNG returned HTTP {exc.code}.") from exc
        except URLError as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError("Cannot reach the local SearXNG service.") from exc
        try:
            payload = json.loads(raw)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG did not return valid JSON.") from exc
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            raise SearchPolicyError("SearXNG returned an invalid JSON object.")
        return payload


class TraceStore:
    def __init__(self, directory: Path | str = "traces"):
        self.directory = Path(directory)

    def record(self, task_id: str, response: SearchResponse) -> Path:
        self.directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        safe_task_id = "".join(char if char.isalnum() or char in "-_" else "_" for char in task_id)[:80] or "default"
        target = self.directory / f"{safe_task_id}.jsonl"
        event = {
            "event_type": "web_search",
            "recorded_at": utc_now(),
            "response": {
                "request_id": response.request_id,
                "query": response.query,
                "retrieved_at": response.retrieved_at,
                "results": [asdict(result) for result in response.results],
            },
        }
        with target.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
        return target


class WebSearchService:
    def __init__(self, client: SearxNGClient, trace_store: TraceStore):
        self.client = client
        self.trace_store = trace_store

    def execute(self, request: SearchRequest, mode: SearchMode, approved_for_task: bool) -> SearchResponse:
        if not user_has_enabled_search(mode, approved_for_task):
            raise SearchUnavailableError("Web search is disabled. Enable it or approve this task before searching.")
        response = self.client.search(request)
        self.trace_store.record(request.task_id, response)
        return response



================================================================================
FILE: probe_model.py
================================================================================

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Probe why the executor model returns no tool call.

Sends the agent's REAL system prompt + REAL tool schemas to Ollama in a few variants
(context size x thinking on/off x short/long task) and prints what comes back.

Run from the project root (Ollama running, no sandbox needed):
    python probe_model.py                       # uses the executor model from config.py
    python probe_model.py --model qwen3:8b      # try another model
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import asyncio
import json
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import httpx

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

from local_agent.config import AgentConfig  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.core.loop import EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.security.permissions import PermissionPolicy  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.browser import (  # noqa: E402
    BrowserClickTool, BrowserGetContentTool, BrowserNavigateTool, BrowserScreenshotTool, BrowserTypeTool,
)
from local_agent.tools.file_ops import ListFilesTool, ReadFileTool, WriteFileTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.python_exec import PythonExecTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.rag import IngestDocumentTool, QueryDocumentsTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.registry import ToolRegistry  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.shell import ShellExecTool  # noqa: E402
from local_agent.tools.web_search import WebSearchTool  # noqa: E402

LONG_GOAL = (
    "Create a complete LAN Network Inventory Dashboard as ONE file /workspace/network_dashboard/index.html "
    "with dark UI, sidebar, three summary cards, a device table of six devices, search, All/Online/Offline "
    "filters, a clear-search button, responsive layout, embedded CSS and JavaScript. Use write_file."
)
SHORT_GOAL = "Create the file /workspace/hello.txt containing the text Hello World. Use write_file."


def build_registry(all_tools: bool) -> ToolRegistry:
    reg = ToolRegistry(PermissionPolicy())
    if all_tools:
        reg.register_many(
            ShellExecTool(), ReadFileTool(), WriteFileTool(), ListFilesTool(), PythonExecTool(),
            WebSearchTool(), BrowserNavigateTool(), BrowserGetContentTool(), BrowserClickTool(),
            BrowserTypeTool(), BrowserScreenshotTool(), IngestDocumentTool(), QueryDocumentsTool(),
        )
    else:
        reg.register(WriteFileTool())
    return reg


async def probe(client, model, goal, ctx, think, all_tools, num_predict):
    reg = build_registry(all_tools)
    system = EXECUTOR_SYSTEM_PROMPT.format(
        tool_names=", ".join(reg.names),
        state_summary=f"Goal: {goal}\nStatus: executing\nStep: 1/2\nCurrent task: {goal}",
    )
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "stream": False,
        "messages": [{"role": "system", "content": system},
                     {"role": "user", "content": f"Execute this step: {goal}"}],
        "tools": reg.get_tool_definitions(),
        "options": {"num_ctx": ctx, "temperature": 0.3, "num_predict": num_predict},
    }
    if think is not None:
        payload["think"] = think
    t0 = time.time()
    r = await client.post("/api/chat", json=payload)
    dt = time.time() - t0
    if r.status_code != 200:
        return {"error": f"HTTP {r.status_code}: {r.text[:200]}", "secs": dt}
    d = r.json()
    m = d.get("message", {})
    return {
        "prompt_tok": d.get("prompt_eval_count"),
        "gen_tok": d.get("eval_count"),
        "done_reason": d.get("done_reason"),
        "content_chars": len(m.get("content", "") or ""),
        "thinking_chars": len(m.get("thinking", "") or ""),
        "tool_calls": [tc.get("function", {}).get("name") for tc in m.get("tool_calls", []) or []],
        "content_head": (m.get("content", "") or "")[:120].replace("\n", " "),
        "secs": dt,
    }


async def main() -> None:
    cfg = AgentConfig.from_env()
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--model", default=cfg.model_roles["executor"].model)
    ap.add_argument("--num-predict", type=int, default=3000)
    args = ap.parse_args()

    variants = [
        # label, goal, ctx, think, all_tools
        ("short task, 1 tool, ctx 8192, think off", SHORT_GOAL, 8192, False, False),
        ("short task, all tools, ctx 8192, think off", SHORT_GOAL, 8192, False, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 4096, think default", LONG_GOAL, 4096, None, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 4096, think off", LONG_GOAL, 4096, False, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 8192, think default", LONG_GOAL, 8192, None, True),
        ("LONG task, all tools, ctx 8192, think off", LONG_GOAL, 8192, False, True),
    ]
    print(f"model = {args.model}   (num_predict cap {args.num_predict})\n")
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(base_url=cfg.ollama_base_url, timeout=600) as client:
        for label, goal, ctx, think, all_tools in variants:
            print(f"--- {label}")
            res = await probe(client, args.model, goal, ctx, think, all_tools, args.num_predict)
            print("   ", json.dumps(res))
    print(
        "\nHow to read this:\n"
        " * prompt_tok close to num_ctx  -> input is being truncated: raise context_length.\n"
        " * done_reason=length + no tool_calls -> ran out of tokens (thinking or a long file body).\n"
        " * thinking_chars large with think default -> thinking is eating the budget: send think=false.\n"
        " * short task fails too -> the model/template isn't producing tool_calls: try another model tag.\n"
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================

# XALGV — Local Autonomous Computer-Using Agent

A local-first, sandboxed, multimodal computer-using agent with dynamic planning, tool execution, browser automation, document intelligence, vision, translation, and custom-model integration.

**One-sentence description:** A local, sandboxed agent that observes a computer environment, plans, acts through tools, verifies results, and adapts — running entirely on your laptop with open-source models served through Ollama.

## Architecture

```
User Goal → Planner → [Observe → Decide → Permission Check → Act → Verify] → Done?
                                                                      ↑              |
                                                                      +-- Adapt/Re-plan ←-+

Host Machine:                          Docker Sandbox:
├── Ollama (GPU, model serving)        ├── FastAPI Tool Server (port 9000)
├── Agent Core (Python)                ├── Xvfb + Openbox (headless GUI)
├── Model Manager (VRAM scheduler)     ├── Firefox/Chromium (browser)
├── Memory (SQLite)                    ├── noVNC (port 6080, watch live)
└── connects over HTTP ───────────────→└── /workspace volume
```

## Quick Start

### 1. Prerequisites

- Python 3.11+
- Docker & Docker Compose
- [Ollama](https://ollama.ai) with `qwen3:8b` (or configure another model)

### 2. Install

```bash
pip install -e ".[dev]"
```

### 3. Start Services

```bash
# Start SearXNG and the sandbox
docker compose up -d

# Pull the default model
ollama pull qwen3:8b
```

### 4. Check Health

```bash
python -m local_agent.main health
```

### 5. Run the Agent

```bash
python -m local_agent.main run "Create a Python hello-world project with tests"
```

### 6. Run Evaluation

```bash
python -m local_agent.main eval
python -m local_agent.main eval --category shell
```

### 7. Web Search

```bash
python -m local_agent.main search "local agent tool calling" --mode on
```

### 8. Watch the Agent Live

Open http://localhost:6080 in your browser to see the sandbox desktop via noVNC.

## Project Structure

```
├── local_agent/                # Main package
│   ├── config.py              # Configuration system
│   ├── models.py              # Ollama model manager with VRAM scheduling
│   ├── main.py                # CLI entry point
│   ├── web_search.py          # SearXNG search integration
│   ├── core/                  # Agent core
│   │   ├── loop.py            # ReAct control loop
│   │   ├── planner.py         # LLM-based plan generation
│   │   ├── verifier.py        # Post-action verification
│   │   └── state.py           # Mutable agent state
│   ├── tools/                 # Tool system
│   │   ├── base.py            # Abstract Tool base class
│   │   ├── registry.py        # Tool registry with permission gating
│   │   ├── shell.py           # Shell execution tool
│   │   ├── file_ops.py        # File read/write/list tools
│   │   └── python_exec.py     # Python code execution tool
│   ├── memory/                # Memory subsystem
│   │   ├── store.py           # SQLite persistent store
│   │   ├── working.py         # Rolling context window
│   │   └── checkpoint.py      # Checkpoint and resume
│   ├── security/              # Security & safety
│   │   ├── permissions.py     # 5-tier permission system
│   │   └── budget.py          # Step/time/repeat budget enforcement
│   └── sandbox/               # Sandbox communication
│       └── client.py          # HTTP client for the tool server
├── sandbox/                   # Docker sandbox definition
│   ├── Dockerfile             # Debian-based Linux workstation
│   ├── tool_server.py         # FastAPI tool server
│   └── entrypoint.sh          # Starts Xvfb, Openbox, noVNC, tool server
├── eval/                      # Evaluation harness
│   ├── harness.py             # Task runner and reporting
│   └── tasks/                 # Task suites (JSON)
│       ├── shell_tasks.json   # 5 shell/filesystem tasks
│       ├── file_tasks.json    # 5 file operation tasks
│       └── python_tasks.json  # 5 Python coding tasks
├── tests/                     # Unit tests
├── searxng/                   # SearXNG config
├── compose.yaml               # Docker Compose for sandbox + SearXNG
└── pyproject.toml             # Project metadata
```

## Design Principles

| Principle | Meaning |
|-----------|---------|
| Dynamic decisions, fixed scaffolding | The loop, tool schemas, permissions, and budgets are fixed and tested |
| Observe before and after acting | Every action is followed by verification |
| Structure first, pixels second | DOM/accessibility trees before screenshots |
| External verification | Check outcomes with code, not LLM judgement |
| Sandbox by default | Agent never runs on the host |
| Local-first | Inference, memory, documents stay local |
| One model at a time | VRAM-aware scheduler swaps models |
| Measure everything | Eval harness runs after every change |

## Permission Tiers

| Tier | Level | Auto-approved | Examples |
|------|-------|---------------|----------|
| READ_ONLY | 0 | ✓ | Read files, list dirs, screenshots |
| WORKSPACE_WRITE | 1 | ✓ | Write/delete in /workspace |
| NETWORK | 2 | ✗ | Web search, fetch pages |
| SYSTEM | 3 | ✗ | Install packages, change config |
| DESTRUCTIVE | 4 | ✗ (always needs human) | rm -rf, drop tables |

## Running Tests

```bash
python -m pytest tests/ -v
```

## Roadmap

- [x] **Phase 0:** Environment, Ollama, tool-calling hello world
- [x] **Phase 1:** Core loop, sandbox, shell/file/Python tools, eval harness
- [ ] **Phase 2:** Browser automation (Playwright), SearXNG integration, RAG
- [ ] **Phase 3:** GUI desktop (Xvfb), VLM, LibreOffice
- [ ] **Phase 4:** Translation, speech, image generation, VRAM scheduler
- [ ] **Phase 5:** Skill library, memory, fine-tuning, red teaming, UI

FILE: run_cjecks.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run health check, unit tests and eval suites; store everything under results/.

Usage (from the project root):
    python run_checks.py                       # everything
    python run_checks.py --tag qwen3-8b        # label the run (shows up in history.csv)
    python run_checks.py --categories shell file
    python run_checks.py --skip-eval           # unit tests + health only
    python run_checks.py --force               # continue even if health fails

Model selection comes from XALGV_* environment variables (see config.py),
so set them before running this script.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
CATEGORIES = ["shell", "file", "python", "browser", "combined"]
PY = sys.executable


def run(cmd: list[str], out_file: Path, timeout: int | None = None) -> int:
    """Run a command, stream output to console and save it to out_file."""
    print(f"\n$ {' '.join(cmd)}")
    with out_file.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        try:
            proc = subprocess.Popen(
                cmd, cwd=ROOT, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
            )
        except FileNotFoundError as exc:
            msg = f"command not found: {exc}\n"
            print(msg)
            fh.write(msg)
            return 127
        start = time.time()
        assert proc.stdout is not None
        for line in proc.stdout:
            sys.stdout.write(line)
            fh.write(line)
            if timeout and time.time() - start > timeout:
                proc.kill()
                fh.write(f"\n[run_checks] killed after {timeout}s timeout\n")
                return 124
        return proc.wait()


def capture(cmd: list[str]) -> str:
    try:
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd, cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
            encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
        ).stdout.strip()
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return f"(unavailable: {exc})"


class VramSampler(threading.Thread):
    """Samples GPU memory used (MiB) via nvidia-smi while a command runs."""

    def __init__(self, interval: float = 2.0) -> None:
        super().__init__(daemon=True)
        self.interval = interval
        self.peak = 0
        self._halt = threading.Event()
        self.available = shutil.which("nvidia-smi") is not None

    def run(self) -> None:
        while self.available and not self._halt.is_set():
            out = capture(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=memory.used", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"])
            try:
                self.peak = max(self.peak, int(out.splitlines()[0].strip()))
            except (ValueError, IndexError):
                pass
            self._halt.wait(self.interval)

    def stop(self) -> int:
        self._halt.set()
        return self.peak


def model_roles() -> dict:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
    try:
        from local_agent.config import AgentConfig

        cfg = AgentConfig.from_env()
        return {
            role: {
                "model": rc.model, "num_ctx": rc.context_length,
                "temperature": rc.temperature, "thinking": rc.thinking,
            }
            for role, rc in cfg.model_roles.items()
        }
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return {"error": str(exc)}


def summarize(report_path: Path) -> dict:
    data = json.loads(report_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    results = data.get("results", [])
    steps = [r.get("steps_taken", 0) for r in results]
    failures = []
    for r in results:
        if r.get("passed"):
            continue
        reason = r.get("error")
        if not reason:
            bad = [c for c in r.get("checker_results", []) if not c.get("passed", False)]
            reason = "; ".join(str(c.get("description") or c.get("path") or c.get("error") or c.get("type")) for c in bad)
        failures.append({"task_id": r.get("task_id"), "steps": r.get("steps_taken"), "reason": reason or "unknown"})
    return {
        "total": data.get("total_tasks", len(results)),
        "passed": data.get("passed", 0),
        "failed": data.get("failed", 0),
        "errors": data.get("errors", 0),
        "success_rate": data.get("success_rate", 0.0),
        "wall_time_s": data.get("wall_time_seconds", 0.0),
        "avg_steps": round(sum(steps) / len(steps), 1) if steps else 0,
        "failures": failures,
    }


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--tag", default="", help="label for this run, e.g. the model name")
    ap.add_argument("--categories", nargs="*", default=CATEGORIES)
    ap.add_argument("--skip-unit", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--skip-eval", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="continue even if health check fails")
    ap.add_argument("--timeout", type=int, default=3600, help="per-category eval timeout (s)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    stamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    out = ROOT / "results" / (stamp + (f"_{args.tag}" if args.tag else ""))
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    print(f"Results directory: {out}")

    # 1. Environment snapshot (so results are reproducible and comparable).
    env = {
        "timestamp": stamp,
        "tag": args.tag,
        "python": sys.version.split()[0],
        "git_commit": capture(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"]),
        "git_dirty": bool(capture(["git", "status", "--porcelain"])),
        "model_roles": model_roles(),
        "ollama_list": capture(["ollama", "list"]),
        "gpu": capture(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=name,memory.total,driver_version", "--format=csv,noheader"]),
        "docker_ps": capture(["docker", "compose", "ps", "--format", "table"]),
    }
    (out / "environment.json").write_text(json.dumps(env, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    summary: dict = {"environment": env, "unit_tests": None, "health": None, "eval": {}}

    # 2. Health (Ollama + sandbox reachable).
    rc = run([PY, "-m", "local_agent.main", "health"], out / "health.txt")
    summary["health"] = "ok" if rc == 0 else "FAILED"
    if rc != 0 and not args.force and not args.skip_eval:
        print("\nHealth check failed: start Ollama and `docker compose up -d`, or use --force.")
        (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        return 2

    # 3. Unit tests.
    if not args.skip_unit:
        rc = run([PY, "-m", "pytest", "tests", "-q", "-p", "no:cacheprovider"], out / "unit_tests.txt")
        tail = (out / "unit_tests.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip().splitlines()
        summary["unit_tests"] = {"exit_code": rc, "result": tail[-1] if tail else ""}

    # 4. Eval suites, one category at a time, with peak VRAM sampling.
    if not args.skip_eval:
        report_src = ROOT / "traces" / "eval_report.json"
        for cat in args.categories:
            if report_src.exists():
                report_src.unlink()  # avoid copying a stale report
            sampler = VramSampler()
            sampler.start()
            t0 = time.time()
            rc = run([PY, "-m", "local_agent.main", "-v", "eval", "--category", cat],
                     out / f"eval_{cat}.log", timeout=args.timeout)
            peak = sampler.stop()
            if report_src.exists():
                shutil.copy(report_src, out / f"eval_{cat}.json")
                s = summarize(out / f"eval_{cat}.json")
            else:
                s = {"total": 0, "passed": 0, "failed": 0, "errors": 0, "success_rate": 0.0,
                     "wall_time_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "avg_steps": 0,
                     "failures": [{"task_id": "-", "steps": 0, "reason": f"no report produced (exit {rc})"}]}
            s["peak_vram_mib"] = peak or None
            s["exit_code"] = rc
            summary["eval"][cat] = s

        db = ROOT / "data" / "agent.db"
        if db.exists():
            shutil.copy(db, out / "agent.db")  # run + step traces

    # 5. Write summary.json, summary.md and append to history.csv.
    (out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")

    lines = [f"# Run {stamp} {args.tag}".strip(), ""]
    lines.append(f"- Health: {summary['health']}")
    if summary["unit_tests"]:
        lines.append(f"- Unit tests: {summary['unit_tests']['result']}")
    lines.append(f"- Models: " + ", ".join(
        f"{r}={v['model']}" for r, v in env["model_roles"].items() if isinstance(v, dict) and r in ("planner", "executor")))
    lines += ["", "| Category | Passed | Rate | Avg steps | Time (s) | Peak VRAM (MiB) |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]
    for cat, s in summary["eval"].items():
        lines.append(f"| {cat} | {s['passed']}/{s['total']} | {s['success_rate']:.0%} | {s['avg_steps']} | "
                     f"{s['wall_time_s']} | {s['peak_vram_mib'] or 'n/a'} |")
    fails = [(c, f) for c, s in summary["eval"].items() for f in s["failures"]]
    if fails:
        lines += ["", "## Failures", ""]
        lines += [f"- [{c}] {f['task_id']} ({f['steps']} steps): {f['reason']}" for c, f in fails]
    (out / "summary.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    hist = ROOT / "results" / "history.csv"
    new = not hist.exists()
    with hist.open("a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        w = csv.writer(fh)
        if new:
            w.writerow(["timestamp", "tag", "git", "executor_model", "category", "passed", "total",
                        "success_rate", "avg_steps", "wall_time_s", "peak_vram_mib"])
        ex = env["model_roles"].get("executor", {}).get("model", "?") if isinstance(env["model_roles"], dict) else "?"
        for cat, s in summary["eval"].items():
            w.writerow([stamp, args.tag, env["git_commit"], ex, cat, s["passed"], s["total"],
                        s["success_rate"], s["avg_steps"], s["wall_time_s"], s["peak_vram_mib"]])

    print("\n" + "\n".join(lines))
    print(f"\nSaved to {out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

================================================================================
FILE: sandbox\browser_server.py

"""Browser automation endpoints for the sandbox tool server.

Uses Playwright to control a browser inside the sandbox container.
Follows the 'structure first, pixels second' principle — the
accessibility tree is the primary interface, screenshots are fallback.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter
from pydantic import BaseModel

router = APIRouter(prefix="/tools/browser", tags=["browser"])

# ── Global browser state ────────────────────────────────────────────────

_browser = None
_page = None


async def _ensure_browser():
    """Lazily initialise the browser and a default page."""
    global _browser, _page
    if _browser is None:
        from playwright.async_api import async_playwright
        pw = await async_playwright().start()
        _browser = await pw.chromium.launch(
            headless=False,  # Visible on the Xvfb display for noVNC viewing.
            args=[
                "--no-sandbox",
                "--disable-gpu",
                "--disable-dev-shm-usage",
                f"--display={os.environ.get('DISPLAY', ':99')}",
            ],
        )
    if _page is None or _page.is_closed():
        context = await _browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width": 1280, "height": 720},
            user_agent="XALGV-Agent/0.1 (autonomous research assistant)",
        )
        _page = await context.new_page()
    return _page


# ── Request / Response models ────────────────────────────────────────────

class BrowserResponse(BaseModel):
    success: bool
    output: str = ""
    error: str | None = None
    artifacts: list[str] = []


class NavigateRequest(BaseModel):
    url: str
    wait_for: str = "load"


class ContentRequest(BaseModel):
    mode: str = "a11y"  # "a11y", "text", "html"
    selector: str | None = None


class ClickRequest(BaseModel):
    selector: str | None = None
    text: str | None = None
    role: str | None = None


class TypeRequest(BaseModel):
    selector: str
    text: str
    clear_first: bool = True
    press_enter: bool = False


class ScreenshotRequest(BaseModel):
    full_page: bool = False
    selector: str | None = None


# ── Endpoints ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@router.post("/navigate", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def navigate(req: NavigateRequest) -> BrowserResponse:
    """Navigate to a URL and return page title + accessible summary."""
    try:
        page = await _ensure_browser()
        response = await page.goto(req.url, wait_until=req.wait_for, timeout=30000)

        title = await page.title()
        url = page.url

        # Get a quick accessibility snapshot for context.
        try:
            a11y = await page.accessibility.snapshot()
            summary = _format_a11y_tree(a11y, max_depth=2, max_items=20)
        except Exception:
            summary = f"Page loaded but accessibility tree unavailable."

        status_code = response.status if response else "unknown"
        output = (
            f"Title: {title}\n"
            f"URL: {url}\n"
            f"Status: {status_code}\n\n"
            f"Page summary:\n{summary}"
        )
        return BrowserResponse(success=True, output=output[:10000])
    except Exception as exc:
        return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Navigation error: {exc}")


@router.post("/get_content", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def get_content(req: ContentRequest) -> BrowserResponse:
    """Get page content as accessibility tree, text, or simplified HTML."""
    try:
        page = await _ensure_browser()

        if req.mode == "a11y":
            snapshot = await page.accessibility.snapshot()
            if snapshot is None:
                return BrowserResponse(success=True, output="[Empty accessibility tree]")
            output = _format_a11y_tree(snapshot, max_depth=5, max_items=100)

        elif req.mode == "text":
            if req.selector:
                element = page.locator(req.selector)
                output = await element.inner_text(timeout=5000)
            else:
                output = await page.inner_text("body", timeout=5000)

        elif req.mode == "html":
            if req.selector:
                element = page.locator(req.selector)
                output = await element.inner_html(timeout=5000)
            else:
                # Return simplified HTML — strip scripts, styles, etc.
                output = await page.evaluate("""() => {
                    const clone = document.body.cloneNode(true);
                    clone.querySelectorAll('script, style, noscript, svg, img').forEach(el => el.remove());
                    return clone.innerHTML.replace(/\\s+/g, ' ').trim();
                }""")
        else:
            return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Unknown mode: {req.mode}")

        return BrowserResponse(success=True, output=output[:20000])
    except Exception as exc:
        return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Content error: {exc}")


@router.post("/click", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def click(req: ClickRequest) -> BrowserResponse:
    """Click an element by selector, text, or role."""
    try:
        page = await _ensure_browser()

        if req.selector:
            await page.click(req.selector, timeout=5000)
            desc = f"Clicked element: {req.selector}"
        elif req.text and req.role:
            await page.get_by_role(req.role, name=req.text).click(timeout=5000)
            desc = f"Clicked {req.role} with text '{req.text}'"
        elif req.text:
            await page.get_by_text(req.text, exact=False).first.click(timeout=5000)
            desc = f"Clicked element with text '{req.text}'"
        else:
            return BrowserResponse(success=False, error="Provide selector, text, or text+role.")

        # Wait briefly for any navigation or DOM updates.
        await page.wait_for_timeout(500)

        title = await page.title()
        return BrowserResponse(success=True, output=f"{desc}\nPage title: {title}\nURL: {page.url}")
    except Exception as exc:
        return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Click error: {exc}")


@router.post("/type", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def type_text(req: TypeRequest) -> BrowserResponse:
    """Type text into an input element."""
    try:
        page = await _ensure_browser()

        if req.clear_first:
            await page.fill(req.selector, "", timeout=5000)

        await page.type(req.selector, req.text, timeout=5000)

        if req.press_enter:
            await page.press(req.selector, "Enter")
            await page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

        return BrowserResponse(
            success=True,
            output=f"Typed '{req.text[:50]}...' into {req.selector}"
                   + (" and pressed Enter" if req.press_enter else ""),
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Type error: {exc}")


@router.post("/screenshot", response_model=BrowserResponse)
async def browser_screenshot(req: ScreenshotRequest) -> BrowserResponse:
    """Take a screenshot of the browser page."""
    try:
        page = await _ensure_browser()
        screenshot_path = "/tmp/browser_screenshot.png"

        if req.selector:
            element = page.locator(req.selector)
            await element.screenshot(path=screenshot_path, timeout=5000)
        else:
            await page.screenshot(path=screenshot_path, full_page=req.full_page)

        return BrowserResponse(
            success=True,
            output=f"Screenshot saved to {screenshot_path}",
            artifacts=[screenshot_path],
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        return BrowserResponse(success=False, error=f"Screenshot error: {exc}")


# ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _format_a11y_tree(
    node: dict[str, Any] | None,
    indent: int = 0,
    max_depth: int = 5,
    max_items: int = 100,
    _count: list[int] | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Format an accessibility tree snapshot into readable text."""
    if node is None:
        return ""
    if _count is None:
        _count = [0]

    if _count[0] >= max_items:
        return ""
    _count[0] += 1

    role = node.get("role", "")
    name = node.get("name", "")
    value = node.get("value", "")

    parts = []
    prefix = "  " * indent
    label = f"{prefix}[{role}]"
    if name:
        label += f" \"{name}\""
    if value:
        label += f" = \"{value}\""
    parts.append(label)

    if indent < max_depth:
        for child in node.get("children", []):
            child_text = _format_a11y_tree(child, indent + 1, max_depth, max_items, _count)
            if child_text:
                parts.append(child_text)

    return "\n".join(parts)

FILE: sandbox\Dockerfile
